     // rows per micro-tile\nconstexpr size_t NR = 8;      // cols per micro-tile (2 x 4 doubles)\nconstexpr size_t KC = 256;    // k-block (packed B panel ~ L1)\nconstexpr size_t MC = 96;     // row block (packed A ~ L2)\nconstexpr size_t NC = 1024;   // column block (packed B ~ L3 share)\n\nstatic inline v4d loadv(const double* p) { v4d v; std::memcpy(&v, p, sizeof(v)); return v; }\nstatic inline void storev(double* p, const v4d v) { std::memcpy(p, &v, sizeof(v)); r < MR; ++r) {\n        if constexpr (First) {\n            c[r][0] = v4d{0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0};\n            c[r][1] = v4d{0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0};\n        } else {\n            c[r][0] = loadv(Ct + r * ldc);\n            c[r][1] = loadv(Ct + r * ldc + 4);\n        }\n    }\n    for (size_t k = 0; c < nr; ++c) Ct[r * ldc + c] = tmp[r * NR + c];\n}\n\nvoid matrixMultiplyLocal(const double* A, const double* B, double* C, const size_t rows, const size_t N) {\n    if (rows == 0 || N == 0) return;\n    std::vector<double> Bpack(KC * (NC + NR));\n    std::vector<double> Apack(KC * (MC + MR));\n\n    for (size_t jc = 0; jc < N; jc += NC) {\n        const size_t nc = std::min(NC, N - jc);\n        for (size_t pc = 0; pc < N; pc += KC) {\n            const size_t kc = std::min(KC, N - pc);\n            const bool first = (pc == 0);\n\n            // Pack B[pc:pc+kc, jc:jc+nc] into NR-wide panels (zero padded)\n            for (size_t jp = 0; jp < nc; jp += NR) {\n                const size_t nr = std::min(NR, nc - jp);\n                double* dst = Bpack.data() + jp * kc;\n                for (size_t k = 0; k < kc; ++k) {\n                    const double* src = B + (pc + k) * N + jc + jp;\n                    size_t c = 0;\n                    for (; c < nr; ++c) dst[k * NR + c] = src[c];\n                    for (; c < NR; ++c) dst[k * NR + c] = 0.0;\n                }\n            }\n\n            for (size_t ic = 0; ic < rows; ic += MC) {\n                const size_t mc = std::min(MC, rows - ic);\n\n                // Pack A[ic:ic+mc, pc:pc+kc] into MR-tall panels (zero padded)\n                for (size_t ip = 0; ip < mc; ip += MR) {\n                    const size_t mr = std::min(MR, mc - ip);\n                    double* dst = Apack.data() + ip * kc;\n                    for (size_t r = 0; r < MR; ++r) {\n                        if (r < mr) {\n                            const double* src = A + (ic + ip + r) * N + pc;\n                            for (size_t k = 0; k < kc; ++k) dst[k * MR + r] = src[k];\n                        } else {\n                            for (size_t k = 0; k < kc; ++k) dst[k * MR + r] = 0.0;\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n\n                for (size_t jp = 0; jp < nc; jp += NR) {\n                    const size_t nr = std::min(NR, nc - jp);\n                    const double* Bp = Bpack.data() + jp * kc;\n                    for (size_t ip = 0; ip < mc; ip += MR) {\n                        const size_t mr = std::min(MR, mc - ip);\n                        const double* Ap = Apack.data() + ip * kc;\n                        double* Ct = C + (ic + ip) * N + jc + jp;\n                        if (first)\n                            tileKernel<true>(kc, Ap, Bp, Ct, N, mr, nr);\n                        else\n                            tileKernel<false>(kc, Ap, Bp, Ct, N, mr, nr);\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Simple validation: compute a single element and compare\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,\n                   const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {\n    // Check a few random positions\n    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};\n    \n    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {\n        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {\n            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;\n            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;\n            \n            double expected = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {\n                expected += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];\n            }\n            \n            const double actual = C[i * N + j];\n            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));\n            \n            if (relError > 1e-6) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n    int rank = 0, nprocs = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &nprocs);\n    const bool root = (rank == 0);\n\n    size_t N = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments (identically on every rank)\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            N = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (root) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (root) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    if (root) {\n        printf(\"Matrix Multiplication Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Matrix size: %zu x %zu\\n\", N, N);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n\n    // 1D block-row distribution of A and C; B is replicated (generated locally, no communication)\n    std::vector<int> rowCounts(nprocs), rowDispls(nprocs);\n    for (int p = 0; p < nprocs; ++p) {\n        const size_t base = N / nprocs, rem = N % nprocs;\n        const size_t up = static_cast<size_t>(p);\n        rowCounts[p] = static_cast<int>(base + (up < rem ? 1 : 0));\n        rowDispls[p] = static_cast<int>(up * base + std::min(up, rem));\n    }\n    const size_t rowBegin = rowDispls[rank];\n    const size_t localRows = rowCounts[rank];\n\n    // Allocate matrices (root holds the full A when validating, and the full C)\n    const size_t aRows = (root && validate) ? N : localRows;\n    std::vector<double> A(aRows * N);\n    std::vector<double> B(N * N);\n    std::vector<double> C(root ? N * N : localRows * N);\n    \n    // Initialize matrices\n    if (root) printf(\"Initializing matrices...\\n\");\n    if (aRows == N) initMatrix(A, N);\n    else initMatrixRows(A, N, rowBegin, rowBegin + localRows);\n    initMatrix(B, N);\n    // A's local rows always start at offset 0 (root owns rows starting at 0)\n    \n    // Row datatype so element counts never overflow int\n    MPI_Datatype rowType;\n    MPI_Type_contiguous(static_cast<int>(N > 0 ? N : 1), MPI_DOUBLE, &rowType);\n    MPI_Type_commit(&rowType);\n\n    // Perform matrix multiplication\n    if (root) printf(\"Computing matrix multiplication...\\n\");\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    \n    matrixMultiplyLocal(A.data(), B.data(), C.data(), localRows, N);\n\n    // Gather distributed C rows onto root\n    if (N > 0) {\n        if (root) {\n            MPI_Gatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, rowType, C.data(), rowCounts.data(), rowDispls.data(),\n                        rowType, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n        } else {\n            MPI_Gatherv(C.data(), static_cast<int>(localRows), rowType, nullptr, nullptr, nullptr,\n                        rowType, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n        }\n    }\n    \n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n    MPI_Type_free(&rowType);\n    \n    int exitCode = 0;\n    if (root) {\n        printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n        \n        // Calculate GFLOPS\n        double gflops = (2.0 * N * N * N) / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n        printf(\"Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\\n\", gflops);\n        \n        // Print results for external validation\n        if (printResults) {\n            print_results(C, \"MatrixC\");\n        }\n        \n        // Validation\n        if (validate) {\n            printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n            bool valid = validateResult(A, B, C, N);\n            \n            if (valid) {\n                printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            } else {\n                printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n                exitCode = 1;\n            }\n        }\n        fflush(stdout);\n    }\n    MPI_Bcast(&exitCode, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    \n    MPI_Finalize();\n    return exitCode;\n}\nEOF\ncat > CMakeLists.txt <<'EOF'\ncmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.10)\nproject(matmul_benchmark)\n\nset(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 20)\nset(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)\n\nfind_package(MPI REQUIRED COMPONENTS CXX)\n\nadd_executable(matmul matmul.cpp)\n\n# Enable optimizations\ntarget_compile_options(matmul PRIVATE -O3 -march=native -Wall -Wextra)\n\n# Link MPI and math library\ntarget_link_libraries(matmul PRIVATE MPI::MPI_CXX m)\nEOF\ncp matmul.cpp /tmp/x.cpp\nmkdir -p /tmp/orig && cd /tmp/orig && cat > /dev/null; echo","is_backgrounded":false,"task_type":"local_bash","uuid":"3ab7ef17-b755-4130-a742-547ba678a275","session_id":"ecaa6c12-249b-4607-857f-5a385eb01aa2"}

     // rows per micro-tile\nconstexpr size_t NR = 8;      // cols per micro-tile (2 x 4 doubles)\nconstexpr size_t KC = 256;    // k-block (packed B panel ~ L1)\nconstexpr size_t MC = 96;     // row block (packed A ~ L2)\nconstexpr size_t NC = 1024;   // column block (packed B ~ L3 share)\n\nstatic inline v4d loadv(const double* p) { v4d v; std::memcpy(&v, p, sizeof(v)); return v; }\nstatic inline void storev(double* p, const v4d v) { std::memcpy(p, &v, sizeof(v)); r < MR; ++r) {\n        if constexpr (First) {\n            c[r][0] = v4d{0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0};\n            c[r][1] = v4d{0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0};\n        } else {\n            c[r][0] = loadv(Ct + r * ldc);\n            c[r][1] = loadv(Ct + r * ldc + 4);\n        }\n    }\n    for (size_t k = 0;      // rows per micro-tile\nconstexpr size_t NR = 8;      // cols per micro-tile (2 x 4 doubles)\nconstexpr size_t KC = 256;    // k-block (packed B panel ~ L1)\nconstexpr size_t MC = 96;     // row block (packed A ~ L2)\nconstexpr size_t NC = 1024;   // column block (packed B ~ L3 share)\n\nstatic inline v4d loadv(const double* p) { v4d v; std::memcpy(&v, p, sizeof(v)); return v; }\nstatic inline void storev(double* p, const v4d v) { std::memcpy(p, &v, sizeof(v)); }\n\n// Ap: packed kc x MR (MR consecutive per k), Bp: packed kc x NR (NR consecutive per k)\n// Ct: MR x NR tile of C with leading dimension ldc\ntemplate <bool First>\nstatic inline void microKernel(const size_t kc, const double* __restrict Ap, const double* __restrict Bp,\n                               double* __restrict Ct, const size_t ldc) {\n    v4d c[MR][2];\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < MR; ++r) {\n        if constexpr (First) {\n            c[r][0] = v4d{0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0};\n            c[r][1] = v4d{0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0};\n        } else {\n            c[r][0] = loadv(Ct + r * ldc);\n            c[r][1] = loadv(Ct + r * ldc + 4);\n        }\n    }\n    for (size_t k = 0; r < MR; ++r) {\n        storev(Ct + r * ldc, c[r][0]);\n        storev(Ct + r * ldc + 4, c[r][1]);\n    }\n}\n\ntemplate <bool First>\nstatic inline void tileKernel(const size_t kc, const double* Ap, const double* Bp,\n                              double* Ct, const size_t ldc, const size_t mr, const size_t nr) {\n    if (mr == MR && nr == NR) {\n        microKernel<First>(kc, Ap, Bp, Ct, ldc);\n        return;\n    }\n    // Edge tile: compute through a full-size scratch tile\n    alignas(64) double tmp[MR * NR] = {};\n    if constexpr (!First) {\n        for (size_t r = 0; jc < N; jc += NC) {\n        const size_t nc = std::min(NC, N - jc);\n        for (size_t pc = 0; pc < N; pc += KC) {\n            const size_t kc = std::min(KC, N - pc);\n            const bool first = (pc == 0);\n\n            // Pack B[pc:pc+kc, jc:jc+nc] into NR-wide panels (zero padded)\n            for (size_t jp = 0; B is replicated (generated locally, no communication)\n    std::vector<int> rowCounts(nprocs), rowDispls(nprocs);\n    for (int p = 0; p < nprocs; ++p) {\n        const size_t base = N / nprocs, rem = N % nprocs;\n        const size_t up = static_cast<size_t>(p);\n        rowCounts[p] = static_cast<int>(base + (up < rem ? 1 : 0));\n        rowDispls[p] = static_cast<int>(up * base + std::min(up, rem));\n    }\n    const size_t rowBegin = rowDispls[rank];\n    const size_t localRows = rowCounts[rank];\n\n    // Allocate matrices (root holds the full A when validating, and the full C)\n    const size_t aRows = (root && validate) ? const size_t ldc) {\n    v4d c[MR][2];\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < MR; ++r) {\n        if constexpr (First) {\n            c[r][0] = v4d{0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0};\n            c[r][1] = v4d{0.0, 0.0, 0.0, size_t mr, const size_t nr) {\n    if (mr == MR && nr == NR) {\n        microKernel<First>(kc, Ap, Bp, Ct, ldc);\n        return;\n    }\n    // Edge tile: compute through a full-size scratch tile\n    alignas(64) double tmp[MR * NR] = {};\n    if constexpr (!First) {\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < mr; ++r)\n            for (size_t c = 0; c < nr; ++c) tmp[r * NR + c] = Ct[r * ldc + c];\n    }\n    microKernel<First>(kc, Ap, Bp, tmp, NR);\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < mr; ++r)\n        for (size_t c = 0; c < nr; ++c) Ct[r * ldc + c] = tmp[r * NR + c];\n}\n\nvoid matrixMultiplyLocal(const double* A, const double* B, double* C, const size_t rows, const size_t N) {\n    if (rows == 0 || N == 0) return;\n    std::vector<double> Bpack(KC * (NC + NR));\n    std::vector<double> Apack(KC * (MC + MR));\n\n    for (size_t jc = 0; jc < N; jc += NC) {\n        const size_t nc = std::min(NC, N - jc);\n        for (size_t pc = 0; pc < N; pc += KC) {\n            const size_t kc = std::min(KC, N - pc);\n            const bool first = (pc == 0);\n\n            // Pack B[pc:pc+kc, jc:jc+nc] into NR-wide panels (zero padded)\n            for (size_t jp = 0; jp < nc; jp += NR) {\n                const size_t nr = std::min(NR, nc - jp);\n                double* dst = Bpack.data() + jp * kc;\n                for (size_t k = 0; k < kc; ++k) {\n                    const double* src = B + (pc + k) * N + jc + jp;\n                    size_t c = 0;\n                    for (; c < nr; ++c) dst[k * NR + c] = src[c];\n                    for (; c < NR; ++c) dst[k * NR + c] = 0.0;\n                }\n            }\n\n            for (size_t ic = 0; ic < rows; ic += MC) {\n                const size_t mc = std::min(MC, rows - ic);\n\n                // Pack A[ic:ic+mc, pc:pc+kc] into MR-tall panels (zero padded)\n                for (size_t ip = 0; ip < mc; ip += MR) {\n                    const size_t mr = std::min(MR, mc - ip);\n                    double* dst = Apack.data() + ip * kc;\n                    for (size_t r = 0; r < MR; ++r) {\n                        if (r < mr) {\n                            const double* src = A + (ic + ip + r) * N + pc;\n                            for (size_t k = 0; k < kc; ++k) dst[k * MR + r] = src[k];\n                        } else {\n                            for (size_t k = 0; k < kc; ++k) dst[k * MR + r] = 0.0;\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n\n                for (size_t jp = 0; jp < nc; jp += NR) {\n                    const size_t nr = std::min(NR, nc - jp);\n                    const double* Bp = Bpack.data() + jp * kc;\n                    for (size_t ip = 0; ip < mc; ip += MR) {\n                        const size_t mr = std::min(MR, mc - ip);\n                        const double* Ap = Apack.data() + ip * kc;\n                        double* Ct = C + (ic + ip) * N + jc + jp;\n                        if (first)\n                            tileKernel<true>(kc, Ap, Bp, Ct, N, mr, nr);\n                        else\n                            tileKernel<false>(kc, Ap, Bp, Ct, N, mr, nr);\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Simple validation: compute a single element and compare\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,\n                   const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {\n    // Check a few random positions\n    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};\n    \n    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {\n        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {\n            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;\n            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;\n            \n            double expected = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {\n                expected += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];\n            }\n            \n            const double actual = C[i * N + j];\n            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));\n            \n            if (relError > 1e-6) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n    int rank = 0, nprocs = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &nprocs);\n    const bool root = (rank == 0);\n\n    size_t N = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments (identically on every rank)\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            N = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (root) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (root) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    if (root) {\n        printf(\"Matrix Multiplication Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Matrix size: %zu x %zu\\n\", N, N);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n\n    // 1D block-row distribution of A and C; B is replicated (generated locally, no communication)\n    std::vector<int> rowCounts(nprocs), rowDispls(nprocs);\n    for (int p = 0; p < nprocs; ++p) {\n        const size_t base = N / nprocs, rem = N % nprocs;\n        const size_t up = static_cast<size_t>(p);\n        rowCounts[p] = static_cast<int>(base + (up < rem ? 1 : 0));\n        rowDispls[p] = static_cast<int>(up * base + std::min(up, rem));\n    }\n    const size_t rowBegin = rowDispls[rank];\n    const size_t localRows = rowCounts[rank];\n\n    // Allocate matrices (root holds the full A when validating, and the full C)\n    const size_t aRows = (root && validate) ? N : localRows;\n    std::vector<double> A(aRows * N);\n    std::vector<double> B(N * N);\n    std::vector<double> C(root ? N * N : localRows * N);\n    \n    // Initialize matrices\n    if (root) printf(\"Initializing matrices...\\n\");\n    if (aRows == N) initMatrix(A, N);\n    else initMatrixRows(A, N, rowBegin, rowBegin + localRows);\n    initMatrix(B, N);\n    // A's local rows always start at offset 0 (root owns rows starting at 0)\n    \n    // Row datatype so element counts never overflow int\n    MPI_Datatype rowType;\n    MPI_Type_contiguous(static_cast<int>(N > 0 ? N : 1), MPI_DOUBLE, &rowType);\n    MPI_Type_commit(&rowType);\n\n    // Perform matrix multiplication\n    if (root) printf(\"Computing matrix multiplication...\\n\");\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    \n    matrixMultiplyLocal(A.data(), B.data(), C.data(), localRows, N);\n\n    // Gather distributed C rows onto root\n    if (N > 0) {\n        if (root) {\n            MPI_Gatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, rowType, C.data(), rowCounts.data(), rowDispls.data(),\n                        rowType, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n        } else {\n            MPI_Gatherv(C.data(), static_cast<int>(localRows), rowType, nullptr, nullptr, nullptr,\n                        rowType, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n        }\n    }\n    \n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n    MPI_Type_free(&rowType);\n    \n    int exitCode = 0;\n    if (root) {\n        printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n        \n        // Calculate GFLOPS\n        double gflops = (2.0 * N * N * N) / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n        printf(\"Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\\n\", gflops);\n        \n        // Print results for external validation\n        if (printResults) {\n            print_results(C, \"MatrixC\");\n        }\n        \n        // Validation\n        if (validate) {\n            printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n            bool valid = validateResult(A, B, C, N);\n            \n            if (valid) {\n                printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            } else {\n                printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n                exitCode = 1;\n            }\n        }\n        fflush(stdout);\n    }\n    MPI_Bcast(&exitCode, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    \n    MPI_Finalize();\n    return exitCode;\n}\nEOF\ncat > CMakeLists.txt <<'EOF'\ncmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.10)\nproject(matmul_benchmark)\n\nset(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 20)\nset(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)\n\nfind_package(MPI REQUIRED COMPONENTS CXX)\n\nadd_executable(matmul matmul.cpp)\n\n# Enable optimizations\ntarget_compile_options(matmul PRIVATE -O3 -march=native -Wall -Wextra)\n\n# Link MPI and math library\ntarget_link_libraries(matmul PRIVATE MPI::MPI_CXX m)\nEOF\ncp matmul.cpp /tmp/x.cpp\nmkdir -p /tmp/orig && cd /tmp/orig && cat > /dev/null; echo"}}

N : localCols;\n    \n    // Initialize matrices\n    if (root) printf(\"Initializing matrices...\\\\n\");\n    initMatrixBlock(A, N, rowBegin, localRows, 0, N);\n    initMatrixBlock(B, N, 0, N, colBegin, localCols);\n    \n    // Perform matrix multiplication\n    if (root) printf(\"Computing matrix multiplication...\\\\n\");\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    \n    matrixMultiplyLocal(A.data(), N, B.data(), localCols, C.data(), ldc, localRows, localCols, N);\n\n    // Collect the distributed C blocks on root (received straight into place)\n    if (root) {\n        std::vector<MPI_Request> reqs;\n        std::vector<MPI_Datatype> types;\n        for (int p = 1; p < nprocs; ++p) {\n            size_t rb, rc, cb, cc;\n            blockRange(Pr, p / Pc, rb, rc);\n            blockRange(Pc, p % Pc, cb, cc);\n            if (rc == 0 || cc == 0) continue;\n            MPI_Datatype blk;\n            MPI_Type_vector(static_cast<int>(rc), static_cast<int>(cc), static_cast<int>(N), MPI_DOUBLE, &blk);\n            MPI_Type_commit(&blk);\n            types.push_back(blk);\n            reqs.emplace_back();\n            MPI_Irecv(C.data() + rb * N + cb, 1, blk, p, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &reqs.back());\n        }\n        MPI_Waitall(static_cast<int>(reqs.size()), reqs.data(), MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);\n        for (auto& t : types) MPI_Type_free(&t);\n    } else if (localRows > 0 && localCols > 0) {\n        MPI_Datatype row;\n        MPI_Type_contiguous(static_cast<int>(localCols), MPI_DOUBLE, &row);\n        MPI_Type_commit(&row);\n        MPI_Send(C.data(), static_cast<int>(localRows), row, 0, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n        MPI_Type_free(&row);\n    }\n    \n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n    \n'''+s[b:]\ns=s.replace('''            printf(\"Validating result...\\\\n\");\n            bool valid''','''            printf(\"Validating result...\\\\n\");\n            // Root only holds its own panels; {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_011CfhZw7tjzv8KVACZA4Jgq","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"The benchmark now always runs under MPI. Only `matmul/matmul.cpp` and `matmul/CMakeLists.txt` changed, and the executable is still `matmul`. Results match the original bit for bit for even N and pass validation at every size and rank count I tried. On this 128-core machine it reaches about 2 TFLOPS at N=8192.\n\n**How it works**\n- **2D process grid:** the ranks are arranged into a near-square grid (`MPI_Dims_create`). Each rank computes one block of C, using its rows of A and its columns of B.\n- **No communication during the multiply:** each rank generates its own part of A and B with the same formula as the original. Per-rank memory and memory traffic are about 2N²/√P rather than about N² for a simple row split. That change took N=8192 on 128 ranks from 830 to about 2,000 GFLOPS.\n- **Faster single-core kernel:** cache-blocked with A and B repacked into small panels, and 6×8 vectorized (AVX2) tiles held in registers.\n- **Collecting the result:** first, ranks in each grid row send their blocks to that row's leader, which builds a full row panel; all rows do this in parallel. Then the leaders send their contiguous panels to rank 0. This replaced sending every block directly to rank 0, which was much slower with Open MPI's default shared-memory transfer.\n- **Program behaviour:** only rank 0 prints. Options, output format, `-r`, `-v` and exit codes behave as before, and every rank returns the same exit code. The timed section includes collecting C on rank 0, so the reported GFLOPS are end-to-end. Message counts use row datatypes, so very large N won't overflow MPI's `int` counts.\n- **CMake:** adds `find_package(MPI)`, links `MPI::MPI_CXX`, and adds `-ffp-contract=off`.\n\n**Correctness.** I compared the result hash against a serial build of the original loop, with N from 1 to 2048 and 1 to 128 ranks, including uneven grids like 13 and 37 ranks.\n- Each element is summed in the same k order as the original, so the result doesn't depend on the rank count.\n- The original compiles to separate multiply and add, so I turned off fused multiply-add (`-ffp-contract=off`). Without it the hashes didn't match. On this CPU the change costs essentially nothing.\n- For odd N (e.g. N=7) the hash differs from the original in the last bit or so. The compiler happens to use one fused multiply-add on the original's final odd k term; I didn't copy that. Validation still passes.\n\n**Performance** (best runs; the machine was shared, load average 70–100, so timings varied by up to about 1.5×):\n\n| Ranks | N=4096 | N=8192 |\n|---|---|---|\n| 1 | 21.7 GFLOPS (original serial loop ≈ 1–2) | – |\n| 64 | ~910 GFLOPS | 1,203 GFLOPS |\n| 128 | ~820 GFLOPS | 1,970–2,110 GFLOPS |\n\nAt N=4096 on 64 or more ranks, collecting the 128 MB result on rank 0 takes about 25–35 ms, roughly a quarter of the run. That limit is set by rank 0 having to receive all of C, not by the multiply.\n\nRun it with e.g. `mpirun --bind-to core -np 128 ./matmul -n 8192`. Without `--oversubscribe`, Open MPI here allows at most 128 ranks, one per physical core."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":265,"cache_read_input_tokens":73276,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":265},"output_tokens":731,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ecaa6c12-249b-4607-857f-5a385eb01aa2","uuid":"2bbb4084-9e41-4a5b-80e1-92926bccf88c","timestamp":"2026-10-04T17:11:16.728Z","request_id":"req_011CfhZw7a9eZdYJEX2HLLxf"}

Options, output format, `-r`, `-v` and exit codes behave as before, and every rank returns the same exit code. The timed section includes collecting C on rank 0, so the reported GFLOPS are end-to-end. Message counts use row datatypes, so very large N won't overflow MPI's `int` counts.\n- **CMake:** adds `find_package(MPI)`, links `MPI::MPI_CXX`, and adds `-ffp-contract=off`.\n\n**Correctness.** I compared the result hash against a serial build of the original loop, with N from 1 to 2048 and 1 to 128 ranks, including uneven grids like 13 and 37 ranks.\n- Each element is summed in the same k order as the original, so the result doesn't depend on the rank count.\n- The original compiles to separate multiply and add, so I turned off fused multiply-add (`-ffp-contract=off`). Without it the hashes didn't match. On this CPU the change costs essentially nothing.\n- For odd N (e.g. N=7) the hash differs from the original in the last bit or so. The compiler happens to use one fused multiply-add on the original's final odd k term; I didn't copy that. Validation still passes.\n\n**Performance** (best runs; the machine was shared, load average 70–100, so timings varied by up to about 1.5×):\n\n| Ranks | N=4096 | N=8192 |\n|---|---|---|\n| 1 | 21.7 GFLOPS (original serial loop ≈ 1–2) | – |\n| 64 | ~910 GFLOPS | 1,203 GFLOPS |\n| 128 | ~820 GFLOPS | 1,970–2,110 GFLOPS |\n\nAt N=4096 on 64 or more ranks, collecting the 128 MB result on rank 0 takes about 25–35 ms, roughly a quarter of the run. That limit is set by rank 0 having to receive all of C, not by the multiply.\n\nRun it with e.g. `mpirun --bind-to core -np 128 ./matmul -n 8192`. Without `--oversubscribe`, Open MPI here allows at most 128 ranks, one per physical core.","ttft_ms":2727,"type":"result","duration_ms":2562776,"uuid":"40295414-74d5-4e08-b598-60761b85ecc7","ttft_stream_ms":1200,"time_to_request_ms":138,"first_content_frame_ms":1200,"queued_turn_count":0,"result_index":0}
